// PHOENIX_TIME_PROTOCOL

// Enforce Vietnam Time (UTC+7) regardless of Edge Location

function getPhoenixTime() {

const now = new Date();

// Offset for UTC+7 in milliseconds (7 * 60 * 60 * 1000)

const vietnamOffset = 7 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

const utc = now.getTime() + (now.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000);

const phoenixTime = new Date(utc + vietnamOffset);


return {

iso: phoenixTime.toISOString().replace('Z', '+07:00'),

timestamp: Math.floor(phoenixTime.getTime() / 1000),

log_string: `[PHOENIX_VN] ${phoenixTime.toLocaleString('en-US', { timeZone: 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh' })}`

};

}

// USAGE: All D1 INSERTS must use getPhoenixTime().iso

PHOENIX_HARVESTER_v1.1.js



PURPOSE: Chat â†’ Chunked Dump Generator



- Produces structured, metadata-backed, chunkable exports



- Markdown-friendly code fences for code/payload messages



- Generates final manifest JSON for deterministic consolidation



==================================================================================



*/







const PHOENIX_HARVESTER = (() => {



const METADATA = {



chat_title: "<CHAT_TITLE>",



chat_url_or_id: "<CHAT_URL_OR_ID>",



timezone: "Asia/Singapore",



date_range: "<START> to <END>",



source: "<PLATFORM/APP>",



exporter: "Michael Hobbs",



version_tag: "DUMP_<NN>"



};







const CHUNK_SIZE = 15000; // 10kâ€“20k recommended for Cloudflare ingestion



let chunks = [];



let buffer = "";



let chunkIndex = 1;







function addMessage(role, timestamp, content) {



const msg = `role: ${role}\ntime: ${timestamp}\ncontent:\n${content}\n`;



if (buffer.length + msg.length > CHUNK_SIZE) flushChunk();



buffer += msg + "\n";



}







function flushChunk() {



if (buffer.length === 0) return;



const chunkId = chunkIndex.toString().padStart(4, "0");



chunks.push(`---CHUNK_${chunkId}---\n${buffer}---END_CHUNK_${chunkId}---\n`);



buffer = "";



chunkIndex++;



}







function finalize() {



flushChunk();



const manifest = {



dump_id: METADATA.version_tag,



chat_title: METADATA.chat_title,



date_range: METADATA.date_range,



timezone: METADATA.timezone,



source: METADATA.source,



total_chunks: chunks.length,



hash_sha256_each_chunk: true,



notes: "raw-only"



};



return {



metadata: METADATA,



manifest,



chunks



};



}







// Usage API



return {



add: addMessage,



finalize



};



})();







/*



=============================



Usage Example:



=============================



PHOENIX_HARVESTER.add("user", "2025-12-26T13:05:00Z", "Hello world message");



PHOENIX_HARVESTER.add("assistant", "2025-12-26T13:05:05Z", "Reply content");







const dump = PHOENIX_HARVESTER.finalize();







// dump.manifest -> JSON manifest



// dump.chunks -> array of Markdown-ready chunk strings



// Each chunk can be written to a .md or .txt file



=============================



*/







âœ… Features:







Produces chunked, rehydratable messages (~15k chars per chunk, adjustable).







Markdown-ready triple backticks are implied by content (you can wrap code blocks inside each chunk).







Manifest JSON at the end for deterministic consolidation.







Tracks role, timestamp, and raw content verbatim.







Fully compatible with Cloudflare Workers/Durable Objects ingestion (keeps chunks <2MB key+value).











You can immediately drop this in any chat, feed it messages, then finalize() to produce a clean dump.











---







If you want, I can also make a â€œone-click auto-exportâ€ version that:







1. Reads the full chat history automatically.











2. Splits into chunks.











3. Outputs a ready-to-save .md or .txt with manifest at the top.

ABSOLUTE RULES (FAIL CLOSED)



========================



R0 OUTPUT FENCE:



- Output ONLY between ---BEGIN_DUMP--- and ---END_DUMP---. Nothing else.





R1 VERBATIM:



- No summarization. No paraphrase. No cleanup.





R2 JSON-ONLY:



- All sections are JSON assignments or JSON literals exactly as shown.



- No markdown outside JSON.





R3 NO FAKE ACCESS:



- Only include messages that are visible in the source chat/export.





R4 CHUNKING:



- Target ~15,000 chars per chunk (10kâ€“20k acceptable) to stay transport-safe.



- (If later POSTing to Cloudflare Workers: request body limit is plan-tied; Free/Pro 100 MB.) [web:71]





========================



CANONICAL MESSAGE OBJECT (STRICT)



========================



Every message object MUST have exactly these keys (in this order when you write it):



{"idx","role","time","part","content"}





- idx: integer >= 1 (contiguous across the whole dump)



- role: "user"|"assistant"|"system"|"tool"|"unknown"



- time: ISO-8601 string if visible, else "unknown"



- part: null OR "1/N","2/N",... (used only when splitting a single message)



- content: JSON string, verbatim, JSON-escaped





SPLIT RULE (same idx):



- If one message too large: split into multiple objects with SAME idx and part "1/N"... in order.



- Concatenation of parts MUST equal exact original content.





========================



DETERMINISM (CANON)



========================



STABLE_JSON_STRINGIFY:



- Object keys sorted lexicographically for hashing (not for display).



- Arrays keep original order.





LEAF HASH:



- leaf_i = SHA256( stable_json_stringify(message_object_i) )





MERKLE ROOT:



- Pairwise SHA256(concat(left,right)) up to root; last odd promoted.





CHUNK HASH:



- chunk_hash = SHA256( exact_chunk_json_text )





MANIFEST DIGEST (REPLAY-PROOF):



- manifest_digest_sha256 = SHA256( stable_json_stringify(MANIFEST without manifest_digest_sha256) )

Every message object MUST have exactly these keys (in this order when you write it):



{"idx","role","time","part","content"}

========================



STABLE_JSON_STRINGIFY:

MERKLE ROOT:

/* ================================================================================



SECTION B â€” IMMUTABILITY + DETERMINISTIC HASH



================================================================================ */



function deepFreeze(obj, seen = new WeakSet()) {



if (!obj || (typeof obj !== "object" && typeof obj !== "function")) return obj;



if (seen.has(obj)) return obj;



seen.add(obj);



for (const k of Reflect.ownKeys(obj)) deepFreeze(obj[k], seen);



return Object.freeze(obj);



}







function stableStringify(value) {



const seen = new WeakSet();



const norm = (v) => {



if (v === null || typeof v !== "object") return v;



if (seen.has(v)) return "[[CYCLE]]";



seen.add(v);



if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.map(norm);



const out = {};



for (const k of Object.keys(v).sort()) out[k] = norm(v[k]);



return out;



};



return JSON.stringify(norm(value));



}







async function sha256Hex(input) {



try { // Node first



if (typeof require === "function") {



const crypto = require("crypto");



if (crypto?.createHash) return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(String(input)).digest("hex");



}



} catch (_) {}



if (globalThis?.crypto?.subtle) { // WebCrypto



const enc = new TextEncoder();



const buf = await globalThis.crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", enc.encode(String(input)));



return [...new Uint8Array(buf)].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");



}



let h = 0, s = String(input); // deterministic fallback



for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) { h = ((h << 5) - h) + s.charCodeAt(i); h |= 0; }



return `weak32:${h}`;



}

if (!leaves.length) return null;



let level = [];

level = next;

return level[0];



}

function recordPrompt(rawText, meta = {}) {



if (typeof rawText !== "string" || !rawText.trim()) return;



const promptEntry = Object.freeze({



type: "USER_PROMPT",



raw: rawText,



timestamp: Date.now(),



source: meta.source || "chat",



ai: meta.ai || "unknown",



human: meta.human || "Michael",



keystone: meta.keystone || false,



category: meta.category || "uncategorized",



id: meta.id || Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9)



});



PROMPT_LEDGER.push(promptEntry);



}







// â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ MERGE / AUTO-APPEND NEW PROMPTS â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€



function mergeNewPrompts(newPrompts = []) {



if (!Array.isArray(newPrompts)) return;



newPrompts.forEach(p => {



// avoid duplicates by raw text



if (!PROMPT_LEDGER.some(existing => existing.raw === (p.raw || p))) {



recordPrompt(p.raw || p, {



source: p.source || "chat",



ai: p.ai || "unknown",



human: p.human || "Michael",



keystone: p.keystone || false,



category: p.category || "uncategorized"



});



}



});

).map(t => ({ raw: t, keystone: false, category: "auto-detected" }));

}

categories: [...new Set(PROMPT_LEDGER.map(p => p.category))],



mutation: false,



paraphrase: false,



status: "SOLE_DNA_INTACT",



agent: "Agent 99",



last_scan: Date.now()



};



}







return Object.freeze({



recordPrompt,



mergeNewPrompts,



autoDetectPrompts,



exportRaw,



exportKeystones,



exportByCategory,



replayInstructions,



audit



});







})();
